### 2026-11-18 — [ARCHITECTURE][SMART DATA][USER DESIGN REVIEW] BK-20261118-ARCH-CENTRAL-GATEWAY — CENTRAL DHAN DATA PIPELINE, PROPOSAL ONLY
- **USER QUESTION / WHY:** Owner asks whether the BOT KING data-management design should expose an always-available Dhan→Oracle pipeline, with BOT KING managing chosen symbols/timeframes/refresh so server edits are infrequent. Request is conceptual architecture evaluation and does NOT itself verify Dhan WebSocket entitlements or authorize an uncontrolled live deployment.
- **SOURCE EVIDENCE:** Reviewed GitHub docs/BOT_KING_REFRESH_DEMAND_INVENTORY_20261116.md and docs/BOT_KING_ORACLE_APP_CONTRACT_20261115.md. Current read-only Android/Oracle have selected 0–70 equity quote route, three independent index quotes, app polls/TTL, server caching plus some single-flight/429 controls, but fully Oracle-owned continuously collected read-only quote/candle/options revision pipeline and central demand coordination are not proven deployed. Repo backend code differs from production /opt/bot-king-server/server.py; NEVER replace production wholesale.
- **OFFICIAL DHAN DOC REVIEW (as of 2026-11-18):** https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/market-quote/ says up to 1000 instruments in a quote REST request, 1 request/second; https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/live-market-feed/ publishes WebSocket subscription support with limits (5 connections/user, 5000 instruments/connection). Dhan streaming packets are event-driven, not a guaranteed one-new-price-per-second per symbol. Option chain and history have different REST pacing and entitlement needs. Source availability does NOT mean unlimited per-second API calls.
- **PROPOSED BEST TARGET:** ONE Dhan authenticated connection/collector owner in Oracle (WebSocket market feed when entitlements and correctness verified; REST quote fallback only within documented bounds), one versioned per-symbol shared read-only cache (LTP, timestamp origin, received/collection age, stale/last-good; 1m/3m/5m candle derivation only from valid evidence), separate 3-index subscription/priority and selected 0/5/51/70 equity set; separate history/expiry/options OI/PCR/max-pain collectors under 429/422, TTL, singleflight; normalized event revisions over authenticated Android↔Oracle stream or conditional delta endpoint, durable reconnect/snapshot resume. BOT KING mobile sends desired saved/visible symbols, timeframe, UI/filter demand; decides local priority and render cadence, NEVER opens direct uncontrolled Dhan fanout. Server enforces entitlements, auth, quota, shared cache across 1 or 2 phones; dynamic subscriptions/refcount and revision fanout must dedup user-owned symbols and preserve cache for brief disconnect.
- **DESIGN TRADEOFF / IMPORTANT CORRECTION:** An open pipeline should be an ALWAYS REACHABLE AUTHENTICATED GATEWAY, not an unlimited Dhan proxy. Oracle owns upstream collection and broker protection; BOT KING owns UI priorities, selection and data-only refresh. A local 1–2 second render observation never promises new exchange data. News is distinct RSS, futures/options feeds must be independently validated, five scoring filters never invent signals, sixth filter remains blank. Keep market 09:10–15:45 IST auto window and after-hours manual data-only refresh; WebSocket keepalive/reconnect should be separated from allowed market-data polling rules.
- **RELEASE PLAN / GATES:** No broad server rewrite before finishing current app source CI. (1) Collect read-only Oracle runtime evidence for current WebSocket permission, Dhan entitlement, existing collector routes, CPU/RAM/memory, currently deployed SHAs/rate-limit, and baseline request count/candle staleness. (2) Implement staged isolated gateway module, behind safe feature flag with existing REST unchanged. (3) Prove finite offline synthetic event ingestion, late/out-of-order ticks, stale on disconnect, 0/5/51/70 unique roster + 3 indexes, two-device same-symbol dedup, 429/reconnect/backpressure, closed market and options 422, rollback. (4) Deploy with backups/hash+health+unauth403+authenticated read-only smoke only after proof, then exact-APK regression and device acceptance. No current Oracle deploy, restart, Dhan API call, order, or APK. Current recent #1954 CI was PASS; #1955 was last observed in progress, verify fresh before next code change.
